# Rules 12–14: complete current-list reassessment

Reviewed **2026-10-18 19:46, Europe/Warsaw**. All **975 eligible entries and 173 vetoed entries** are covered. The 301 hard exclusions remain unscored. [Recommended list](RECOMMENDED_BOOKS.md) · [Vetoed list](VETOED_BOOKS.md) · [Calculations](data/deductions_scores_261018.json) · [Amazon evidence](data/amazon_counts_261018.json) · [Validation](data/deductions_validation_261018.json).

## Result and limits

348 totals are determinate under the recorded assessments; 800 remain provisional. Amazon counts were recovered for 370 works; 778 counts remain unknown. 118 historical endpoints remain unresolved. A range is the set of possible totals from those missing inputs, not a statistical confidence interval. Endpoint estimates marked low confidence are judgments about central scope, not verified exact chronology.

Rule 12 applies to 468 works, including the philosophy addition found in the user’s updated AGENTS.md. 269 works receive both thematic deductions. 813 totals are certainly lower than before; 76 determinate totals are unchanged. No new book was nominated, no low-score book was removed, and all prior vetoes remain.

## Rules applied

The user confirmed that rules 11 and 12 add separately and that age runs from the period’s **end**, with fractional years/25. The ten positive criteria retain their latest recorded attainment. This is a preference-rule reassessment, not a new full-text review of 1,148 works. Prior positive-score reasons, eligibility decisions and veto evidence are linked for each work. No new evidence warranted a change to those ten assessments in this review. Rule 11 is retained from the prior profile; rule 12 is newly assessed.

```text
positive = E²/20 + S²/17 + U + M + D²/10 + R + F + A + N + P
total = positive + T11 + T12 + H13 + O14
T11, T12 each belong to {0, −10}
H13 = −min(12, max(0, 2026 − assessed_period_end)/25)
O14 = −20 if n < 10; −10 if 10 ≤ n < 100; −5 if 100 ≤ n < 1000; 0 otherwise
```

E/S/D in the formula are raw linear assessments, not contributions already squared. The current maxima are 20/17/15/12/10/9/7/5/3/2. An old date of publication or composition does not create an age deduction for a general argument. Broad cross-period syntheses receive H13=0 when no single period is central. A biography or event narrative is assessed from its central coverage, not automatically from the person’s death or a later epilogue. Estimated endpoints remain explicitly low confidence.

Rule 12 concerns the book’s center: finance, war, politics/political intelligence, media, power or philosophy. Technology, engineering, production, logistics, resource management and scientific improvement can use those subjects as applications without receiving the deduction. A category overlap receives rule 12 only once. These are interpretive judgments with moderate confidence unless a note states otherwise.

Unknown O14 is bounded by [−20, 0]; unknown H13 by [−12, 0]. Unknown does not mean no penalty, zero ratings, or an assumed average count. Totals may be negative; no unrequested zero floor is imposed. All calculations and thresholds use unrounded values. General bands remain Recommend ≥85, Consider ≥75, Selective ≥65, Low priority ≥50, Poor fit <50. Vetoes override every band.

## Evidence coverage

Matching direct Amazon product locators were checked for 1,030 works. Another 118 received title-and-author searches. Amazon.de fallback was checked for 160 inaccessible entries and recovered no counts; further repeated blocked-page probes were not treated as evidence. Available Amazon.com aggregates are used consistently, without adding editions, formats or marketplaces. These are cached page snapshots retrieved on 2026-10-18, with crawl-age notes in the evidence file, not guaranteed live counts. One plain-text page supplied a global-rating count; the other recovered counts use the product’s rating aggregate element. Missing pages, missing rating controls and a failed search never establish zero ratings. Related-product ratings and Goodreads totals are not substituted.

Prior Goodreads/other-reader snapshots and edition locators are retained when no Amazon count was observed; they are not newly verified stock or availability claims. All previously conditional identities and edition limits still apply. In particular, named series remain one source reading unit; no invented series-wide Amazon total is assigned.

## Verification

Validated all 1,148 title/author identities, ten raw criterion bounds and contribution calculations, old-total reconciliation, independent new-score bounds, deduction thresholds at 9/10/99/100/999/1000, fractional age arithmetic and the −12 cap, source-number preservation, status-list separation, all 301 exclusions, ranking order, and historical-file hashes. Counts and hashes are stored in the validation file. Missing external inputs remain visible rather than being declared verified.
